"""V15 M6b -- Copyability-Adjusted FINAL Ranking (post-engine).

Design (FROZEN): brain projects/quant/v15/modules/m06b (codex DESIGN-SHIP r3, Alberto greenlit
2026-06-01). This module is the ONLY ranking that locks the V15 investable pool. It re-ranks the
M6a high-recall shortlist on M7 AFTER-OUR-COST engine results computed FOLD-PRETEST-PURE on
[train_start[k], test_start[k]) -- no OOS leak -- via a fully pre-registered spec, and emits the
per-fold INVESTABLE POOL + a QUALITY-only weight. Raw source ROE never locks the pool (thesis 4b).

SIZING CHAIN (one multiplier per module, applied in M9):
  final_slice = quality_weight(M6b) x confidence(M4 alloc) x survival(M8 tier) x caps/anti-corr(M9)
M6b owns ONLY quality_weight (does NOT multiply M4/M8 -- no double-count).

PROVISIONAL vs FINAL (design §1): an M6b run on uncalibrated costs (slippage_uncalibrated or
fee_unversioned True on its M7 input) is stamped investable=False (pipeline dry-run only). The FINAL
investable pool reruns AFTER cost calibration, recording fee_schedule_version +
slippage_calibration_version in the manifest.

Run:
  /Users/hermes/miniforge3/envs/quants-lab/bin/python research/v15/v15_m06b_ranking.py \
      --m07-dir app/data/v15/m07_pretest --out app/data/v15
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger("m06b")

DATA_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15"
MS_PER_DAY = 86_400_000

# Pre-registered min CLOSED round-trips for an entity to be RANKABLE / pool-eligible (codex
# finding): M7 emits realized_roe=0.0 / round_trip_win_rate=0.0 when n_round_trips==0, so an
# open-only entity (zero closed round-trips) would otherwise look valid and rank/pool. The rank
# basis is the after-cost REALIZED round-trip return; without enough closed round-trips that basis
# is undefined/meaningless. Require >= this many closed round-trips to enter in_pool/quality_weight.
MIN_ROUND_TRIPS = 5


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# FROZEN MANIFEST (design §3/§4/§5). Every constant here is pre-registered and
# written verbatim into m06b_manifest.json BEFORE any OOS/M9/M10 run.
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class M6bManifest:
    manifest_version: str = "m06b-v2"
    # Score coefficients (relative importances; NOT required to sum to 1 -- mixed scales).
    # v2: return basis switched to M7 REALIZED round-trip ROE (was full-window MTM roe_engine);
    #     win-rate term ADDED. New positive-weight vector sums to 0.95 (1.00 - 0.15 - 0.10 net of
    #     the subtracted survivability penalty kept at its prior magnitude):
    #       0.25 z_realized_roe + 0.20 z_calmar + 0.15 z_win_rate + 0.15 consistency
    #       + 0.10 capacity_health + 0.10 fidelity - 0.15 survivability_penalty
    w_realized_roe: float = 0.25
    w_calmar: float = 0.20
    w_win_rate: float = 0.15
    w_consistency: float = 0.15
    w_capacity_health: float = 0.10
    w_fidelity: float = 0.10
    w_survivability_penalty: float = 0.15  # subtracted
    # calmar / winsor / fidelity
    dd_floor: float = 0.05            # calmar = realized_roe / max(max_dd, dd_floor)
    winsor_lo_pct: float = 1.0        # winsorize calmar + realized_roe at fold [p1, p99]
    winsor_hi_pct: float = 99.0
    fidelity_B: float = 0.25          # fidelity = 1 - clamp(tracking_error / B, 0, 1)  (M7 D5)
    # consistency sub-split geometry (design §3): consecutive 14d blocks FROM train_start; trailing
    # partial (<14d) DROPPED; sub-split ACTIVE iff >=1 journey opened AND >=5 fills in it.
    block_days: int = 14
    consistency_active_min_journeys: int = 1
    consistency_active_min_fills: int = 5
    consistency_min_active_subsplits: int = 2  # < this -> consistency = 0, not top-bucket eligible
    # min-support to be RANKABLE
    min_fills_pretest: int = 30
    min_exposure_days: float = 3.0
    min_active_subsplits_support: int = 2
    # pool selection + G5
    n_pool: int = 100
    g5_min_active_pretest_folds: int = 3
    g5_min_journeys_pretest: int = 5
    # allocation -- quality weight only
    bucket_weights: tuple = (5, 4, 3, 2, 1)   # quintile buckets, normalized to sum 1
    n_buckets: int = 5
    top_bucket_consistency_gate: float = 0.5  # bucket-1 entity with consistency < this -> demote 1
    per_entity_quality_ceiling: float = 0.10  # pre-M9 ceiling = 10% of pool total
    # provenance / provisional
    informed_by: str = "pretest-distribution-only"
    fee_schedule_version: Optional[str] = None
    slippage_calibration_version: Optional[str] = None


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# INPUT LOADING (design §2: fold-pretest-pure; provenance fail-closed)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _read_parquet_maybe_parts(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Read a parquet that may be a single file OR a `<name>.parts/` dir of shards (streaming I/O)."""
    parts = path.parent / (path.name + ".parts")
    if path.exists() and path.is_file():
        return pd.read_parquet(path)
    if parts.exists() and parts.is_dir():
        shards = sorted(parts.glob("*.parquet"))
        if not shards:
            return pd.DataFrame()
        return pd.concat((pd.read_parquet(s) for s in shards), ignore_index=True)
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"neither {path} nor {parts} exists")


def _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir: Path, folds: pd.DataFrame, stem: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    parts = []
    for fid in sorted(folds["fold_id"].astype(int).unique()):
        p = m04_dir / f"{stem}_f{fid}.parquet"
        if not p.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"missing fold-pure M4 file for fold {fid}: {p}")
        df = pd.read_parquet(p).copy()
        df["fold_id"] = int(fid)
        parts.append(df)
    return pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True) if parts else pd.DataFrame()


def load_inputs(m07_dir: Path, data_dir: Path = DATA_DIR, m04_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict:
    """Load the M7 pretest-window engine results + upstream M6a/M5/M4/M3 inputs.

    Returns a dict of DataFrames. Provenance fail-closed checks happen in build_ranking once we
    know each fold's test_start.
    """
    folds = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m03_folds.parquet")

    m07_summary = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(m07_dir / "m07_summary.parquet")
    # m07_fills only needed for exposure_time (per (entity,fold) our_ts span). Read lazily/aggregated.
    fills_path = m07_dir / "m07_fills.parquet"

    m06a = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m06a_shortlist.parquet")
    m05 = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m05_eligibility.parquet")
    if m04_dir is None:
        logger.warning("LOUD WARNING: using single M4 authenticity/entities files for all folds; "
                       "M6b M4 consumption is not fold-pure. Pass --m04-dir.")
        m04_entities = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m04_entities.parquet")
        # wallet -> entity_id map (for per-block journey-open counts from m02 when equity is present)
        m04_auth = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m04_authenticity.parquet")
        m04_fold_pure = False
    else:
        m04_entities = _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir, folds, "m04_entities")
        m04_auth = _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir, folds, "m04_authenticity")
        m04_fold_pure = True
    m02_journeys_path = data_dir / "m02_journeys.parquet"

    # optional: per-action equity path (FINAL consistency source). Absent in current shipped runner.
    eq_path = m07_dir / "m07_equity.parquet"
    m07_equity = None
    try:
        m07_equity = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(eq_path)
        if m07_equity.empty:
            m07_equity = None
    except FileNotFoundError:
        m07_equity = None

    return {
        "folds": folds,
        "m07_summary": m07_summary,
        "m07_fills_path": fills_path,
        "m06a": m06a,
        "m05": m05,
        "m04_entities": m04_entities,
        "m04_auth": m04_auth,
        "m04_fold_pure": m04_fold_pure,
        "m02_journeys_path": m02_journeys_path,
        "m07_equity": m07_equity,
    }


def _exposure_days_from_fills(fills_path: Path, m07_dir: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per (entity_id, fold_id) exposure_time in days = (max our_ts - min our_ts)/day, from the M7
    fills stream. Single streamed groupby; reads only the needed columns."""
    df = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(fills_path)
    if df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["entity_id", "fold_id", "exposure_days"])
    g = df.groupby(["entity_id", "fold_id"])["our_ts"].agg(["min", "max"]).reset_index()
    g["exposure_days"] = (g["max"] - g["min"]) / MS_PER_DAY
    return g[["entity_id", "fold_id", "exposure_days"]]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# CONSISTENCY (design §3): per-sub-split (14d block from train_start) ENGINE after-cost ROE.
# FINAL source = m07_equity per-block ROE. PROVISIONAL fallback = M6a source persistence (clearly
# flagged non-final; provisional outputs are investable=False anyway).
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _blocks_activity(fills_path, m02_journeys_path: Path, m04_auth: pd.DataFrame,
                     folds: pd.DataFrame, m: M6bManifest) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per (entity_id, fold_id, block_idx) -> n_fills + n_journeys, for the consistency activeness
    test (active iff >= min_journeys opened AND >= min_fills in the block). Blocks are consecutive
    14d windows FROM train_start within the pretest [train_start, test_start); trailing partial
    (block_idx >= n_full) is excluded by the consumer. Only computed when m07_equity is present
    (FINAL path); the provisional path uses M6a source persistence and does not call this.

    fills_path: m07 fills (entity_id, fold_id, our_ts) -> per-block n_fills (engine fills).
    m02 journeys (wallet, entry_ts) mapped to entity via m04_auth (wallet->entity_id) -> per-block
    journey OPENS (entry_ts in block), assigned to every fold whose pretest window contains entry_ts.
    """
    block_ms = m.block_days * MS_PER_DAY
    fold_win = [(int(r.fold_id), pd.Timestamp(r.train_start).value // 1_000_000,
                 pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000) for r in folds.itertuples()]

    # --- fills per (entity, fold, block) ---
    fdf = fills_path if isinstance(fills_path, pd.DataFrame) else _read_parquet_maybe_parts(fills_path)
    f_rows = []
    if not fdf.empty:
        fdf = fdf[["entity_id", "fold_id", "our_ts"]].copy()
        for fid, t0, t1 in fold_win:
            sub = fdf[fdf.fold_id == fid]
            if sub.empty:
                continue
            blk = ((sub["our_ts"] - t0) // block_ms).astype(int)
            sub = sub.assign(block_idx=blk)
            sub = sub[(sub["our_ts"] >= t0) & (sub["our_ts"] < t1)]
            g = sub.groupby(["entity_id", "block_idx"]).size().reset_index(name="n_fills_block")
            g["fold_id"] = fid
            f_rows.append(g)
    fills_blocks = (pd.concat(f_rows, ignore_index=True) if f_rows
                    else pd.DataFrame(columns=["entity_id", "block_idx", "n_fills_block", "fold_id"]))

    # --- journey opens per (entity, fold, block) from m02 entry_ts ---
    has_fold_m04 = "fold_id" in m04_auth.columns
    if has_fold_m04:
        w2e_by_fold = {int(fid): g[["wallet", "entity_id"]].drop_duplicates("wallet")
                       for fid, g in m04_auth.groupby("fold_id")}
        w2e = None
    else:
        w2e = m04_auth[["wallet", "entity_id"]].drop_duplicates("wallet")
        w2e_by_fold = {}
    j_rows = []
    try:
        jdf = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(Path(m02_journeys_path)) if not isinstance(
            m02_journeys_path, pd.DataFrame) else m02_journeys_path
        validity = {"lifecycle_valid", "stream_replay_valid"}
        if not validity.issubset(jdf.columns):
            if not isinstance(m02_journeys_path, pd.DataFrame):
                raise ValueError(
                    "M02 journeys lack lifecycle/stream replay validity; rebuild M2"
                )
        else:
            jdf = jdf[
                jdf["lifecycle_valid"].fillna(False).astype(bool)
                & jdf["stream_replay_valid"].fillna(False).astype(bool)
            ].copy()
        jdf = jdf[["wallet", "entry_ts"]].copy()
        for fid, t0, t1 in fold_win:
            mapper = w2e_by_fold.get(int(fid), pd.DataFrame(columns=["wallet", "entity_id"])) \
                if has_fold_m04 else w2e
            sub = jdf[(jdf["entry_ts"] >= t0) & (jdf["entry_ts"] < t1)].merge(
                mapper, on="wallet", how="inner")
            if sub.empty:
                continue
            blk = ((sub["entry_ts"] - t0) // block_ms).astype(int)
            sub = sub.assign(block_idx=blk, fold_id=fid)
            g = sub.groupby(["entity_id", "fold_id", "block_idx"]).size().reset_index(

 succeeded in 0ms:
                name="n_journeys_block")
            j_rows.append(g)
    except (FileNotFoundError, KeyError):
        pass
    journeys_blocks = (pd.concat(j_rows, ignore_index=True) if j_rows
                       else pd.DataFrame(columns=["entity_id", "fold_id", "block_idx", "n_journeys_block"]))

    out = fills_blocks.merge(journeys_blocks, on=["entity_id", "fold_id", "block_idx"], how="outer")
    out["n_fills_block"] = out["n_fills_block"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    out["n_journeys_block"] = out["n_journeys_block"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    return out


def _consistency_from_equity(equity: pd.DataFrame, folds: pd.DataFrame,
                             m: M6bManifest, blocks_activity: Optional[pd.DataFrame]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """consistency = (# active sub-splits with block roe_adj>0) / (# active sub-splits).
    A sub-split is ACTIVE iff >= min_journeys opened AND >= min_fills in it. Block roe_adj = engine
    after-cost ROE on that block = eq[block_end]/eq[block_start] - 1.

    NOTE: activeness needs per-block journey + fill counts. fills_blocks (per entity,fold,block ->
    n_fills, n_journeys) supplies them; when unavailable we fall back to fill-count-only activeness
    from the equity sample density (conservative: still requires the block to carry >=min_fills
    equity-action samples).
    """
    rows = []
    fold_win = {int(r.fold_id): (pd.Timestamp(r.train_start).value // 1_000_000,
                                 pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000)
                for r in folds.itertuples()}
    block_ms = m.block_days * MS_PER_DAY
    # (entity, fold, block) -> (n_fills_block, n_journeys_block) for the activeness test.
    ba = {}
    keys_act = set()
    if blocks_activity is not None and not blocks_activity.empty:
        for r in blocks_activity.itertuples():
            ba[(int(r.entity_id), int(r.fold_id), int(r.block_idx))] = (
                int(r.n_fills_block), int(r.n_journeys_block))
            keys_act.add((int(r.entity_id), int(r.fold_id)))
    # equity grouped for O(1) lookup; iterate the UNION of (entity,fold) from blocks_activity AND
    # equity (codex r3 #1: an entity active by fills+journeys but with NO equity rows must still get
    # active=N, positive=0, consistency=0 -- fail-closed, never silently absent).
    eq_groups = {(int(k[0]), int(k[1])): g for k, g in equity.groupby(["entity_id", "fold_id"])} \
        if not equity.empty else {}
    all_keys = sorted(keys_act | set(eq_groups.keys()))  # sorted -> deterministic row order
    for (eid, fid) in all_keys:
        if int(fid) not in fold_win:
            continue
        t0, t1 = fold_win[int(fid)]
        grp = eq_groups.get((int(eid), int(fid)))
        anchor_ts = anchor_eq = None
        if grp is not None and not grp.empty:
            grp = grp.sort_values("ts")
            ts = grp["ts"].to_numpy()
            eq = grp["subaccount_equity"].to_numpy()
            # D4: per-block ROE uses the EXACT boundary anchors (fold_start/block_boundary/fold_end),
            # via nearest-at-or-before lookup -- NOT within-block first/last (which excludes the exact
            # block-end sample). Fall back to within-block only when no anchors exist (synthetic equity).
            if "event_flag" in grp.columns:
                am = grp["event_flag"].isin(["fold_start", "block_boundary", "fold_end"]).to_numpy()
                if am.any():
                    anchor_ts = ts[am]
                    anchor_eq = eq[am]
        else:
            ts = np.empty(0, dtype="int64")
            eq = np.empty(0, dtype="float64")
        # consecutive 14d blocks FROM train_start; drop trailing partial (<14d). The ACTIVE
        # denominator is driven by _blocks_activity (fills+journeys), NOT by equity-sample presence
        # (codex r2 #A: skipping equity-empty blocks biased consistency upward). A block that is
        # active by nf/nj counts in the denominator; it is POSITIVE only if equity actually covers
        # it AND its after-cost block ROE > 0 (an active block with no equity coverage = no
        # measurable positive ROE = NOT positive; conservative, no leak).
        n_full = int((t1 - t0) // block_ms)
        active = 0
        positive = 0
        for b in range(n_full):
            nf, nj = ba.get((int(eid), int(fid), b), (0, 0))
            if nf < m.consistency_active_min_fills or nj < m.consistency_active_min_journeys:
                continue  # block not active -> excluded from BOTH numerator and denominator
            active += 1
            b0 = t0 + b * block_ms
            b1 = t0 + (b + 1) * block_ms
            if anchor_ts is not None and anchor_ts.size:
                # D4: anchor-to-anchor block ROE = eq_anchor(b1)/eq_anchor(b0) - 1.
                i0 = int(np.searchsorted(anchor_ts, b0, side="right")) - 1
                i1 = int(np.searchsorted(anchor_ts, b1, side="right")) - 1
                if i0 >= 0 and i1 >= 0:
                    e0, e1 = float(anchor_eq[i0]), float(anchor_eq[i1])
                    if e0 > 0 and (e1 / e0 - 1.0) > 0:
                        positive += 1
            else:
                mask = (ts >= b0) & (ts < b0 + block_ms)
                if mask.any():
                    eq_in = eq[mask]
                    if eq_in[0] > 0 and (eq_in[-1] / eq_in[0] - 1.0) > 0:
                        positive += 1
        cons = 0.0 if active < m.consistency_min_active_subsplits else positive / active
        rows.append({"entity_id": int(eid), "fold_id": int(fid),
                     "consistency": cons, "n_active_subsplits": active,
                     "consistency_source": "m07_equity_block_roe"})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def _consistency_provisional(m06a: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """PROVISIONAL fallback when m07_equity is absent. Uses M6a source persistence
    (active_blocks/possible_blocks) as a SOURCE proxy for cross-sub-split repeatability. Flagged
    non-final: provisional M6b is investable=False, so this never reaches LP/live evidence."""
    df = m06a[["entity_id", "fold_id", "active_blocks", "possible_blocks"]].copy()
    poss = df["possible_blocks"].clip(lower=1)
    df["consistency"] = (df["active_blocks"] / poss).clip(0, 1).fillna(0.0)
    df["n_active_subsplits"] = df["active_blocks"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    df["consistency_source"] = "m6a_source_persistence_PROVISIONAL"
    return df[["entity_id", "fold_id", "consistency", "n_active_subsplits", "consistency_source"]]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# SCORING
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _winsorize(s: pd.Series, lo_pct: float, hi_pct: float) -> pd.Series:
    if s.notna().sum() == 0:
        return s
    lo = np.nanpercentile(s, lo_pct)
    hi = np.nanpercentile(s, hi_pct)
    return s.clip(lower=lo, upper=hi)


def _zscore(s: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    mu = s.mean()
    sd = s.std(ddof=0)
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd == 0:
        return pd.Series(np.zeros(len(s)), index=s.index)
    return (s - mu) / sd


def build_ranking(inputs: dict, m: M6bManifest) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """Core M6b pipeline. Returns (pool_df, manifest_dict)."""
    folds = inputs["folds"]
    summ = inputs["m07_summary"].copy()
    m06a = inputs["m06a"].copy()
    m05 = inputs["m05"].copy()
    m04e = inputs["m04_entities"].copy()

    # --- provenance: per-fold pretest-pure. M6a/M5 carry as_of_ms; refuse any input stamped AFTER
    #     test_start[k] (fixes r1#9 -- no inherited survivor bias / no leak). ---
    fold_test_start_ms = {int(r.fold_id): pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000
                          for r in folds.itertuples()}
    provenance_fail = []
    prov_inputs = [("m06a", m06a), ("m05", m05)]
    if inputs.get("m04_fold_pure"):
        prov_inputs.extend([("m04_entities", m04e), ("m04_auth", inputs["m04_auth"])])
    for nm, df in prov_inputs:
        if "as_of_ms" not in df.columns:
            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:no_as_of_ms_column")
            continue
        if nm.startswith("m04") and "fold_id" not in df.columns:
            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:no_fold_id_column")
            continue
        # require PRESENT + non-null as_of_ms for every row (missing provenance is not provenance-OK)
        n_null = int(df["as_of_ms"].isna().sum())
        if n_null:
            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{n_null}_rows_null_as_of_ms")
        ts_start = df["fold_id"].map(fold_test_start_ms)
        if ts_start.isna().any():
            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(ts_start.isna().sum())}_rows_unknown_fold")
        if nm.startswith("m04"):
            bad = (df["as_of_ms"].astype("float64") != ts_start.astype("float64")).fillna(False)
            if bad.any():
                provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(bad.sum())}_rows_as_of_not_equal_test_start")
        else:
            bad = (df["as_of_ms"].astype("float64") > ts_start.astype("float64")).fillna(False)
            if bad.any():
                provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(bad.sum())}_rows_post_test_start")
    if provenance_fail:
        raise ValueError(f"M6b PROVENANCE FAIL-CLOSED: {provenance_fail}")

    # --- engine after-cost results per (entity,fold) ---
    summ = summ.rename(columns={"max_dd": "max_dd_pretest"})
    summ["capacity_capped_frac"] = summ["n_capacity_capped"] / summ["n_actions"].clip(lower=1)
    summ["account_ruin"] = summ["ruin"].astype(int)
    # uncalibrated-cost flags -> drives provisional/FINAL
    uncalibrated = bool(summ["slippage_uncalibrated"].any() or summ["fee_unversioned"].any())

    # --- RETURN BASIS (v2): rank on M7 REALIZED round-trip ROE, NOT full-window MTM roe_engine.
    #     The M7 engine emits realized_roe over CLEAN round-trips (realized_pnl_total / start_equity).
    #     Fall back to roe_engine ONLY when realized_roe is absent, flagged per-run + per-row so a
    #     fallback never silently masquerades as the realized basis. ---
    has_realized = "realized_roe" in summ.columns
    if has_realized:
        realized = pd.to_numeric(summ["realized_roe"], errors="coerce")
        summ["roe_adj"] = realized
        # rows where realized_roe is NaN but roe_engine exists fall back per-row (flagged)
        if "roe_engine" in summ.columns:
            fb_mask = summ["roe_adj"].isna() & summ["roe_engine"].notna()
            summ.loc[fb_mask, "roe_adj"] = pd.to_numeric(
                summ.loc[fb_mask, "roe_engine"], errors="coerce")
        summ["return_basis"] = np.where(realized.notna(), "realized_roe", "roe_engine_fallback")
        return_basis_fallback = bool((summ["return_basis"] == "roe_engine_fallback").any())
    else:
        summ["roe_adj"] = pd.to_numeric(summ.get("roe_engine"), errors="coerce")
        summ["return_basis"] = "roe_engine_fallback"
        return_basis_fallback = True
    # round-trip win-rate term (v2). Absent -> NaN (treated as missing realized metrics downstream).
    if "round_trip_win_rate" in summ.columns:
        summ["round_trip_win_rate"] = pd.to_numeric(summ["round_trip_win_rate"], errors="coerce")
    else:
        summ["round_trip_win_rate"] = np.nan
    # whether the engine actually reported closed round-trips (drives the MIN_ROUND_TRIPS gate).
    # When the column is entirely absent (legacy / pure roe_engine provisional input -- no realized
    # round-trip accounting at all), the gate cannot apply; an open-only entity in a REAL M7 v2 run
    # always carries n_round_trips (0 for open-only) and is therefore gated.
    has_n_round_trips = "n_round_trips" in summ.columns
    for c in ("n_round_trips", "n_round_trip_wins", "realized_pnl_total"):
        if c not in summ.columns:
            summ[c] = np.nan

    # --- exposure_time from fills (min-support) ---
    exposure = _exposure_days_from_fills(inputs["m07_fills_path"], None)

    # --- consistency. FINAL path = M7 equity per-block ROE (activeness from per-block fills+journeys).
    #     PROVISIONAL path (no equity) = M6a source persistence, flagged non-final. ---
    if inputs["m07_equity"] is not None:
        blocks_act = _blocks_activity(inputs["m07_fills_path"], inputs["m02_journeys_path"],
                                      inputs["m04_auth"], folds, m)
        cons = _consistency_from_equity(inputs["m07_equity"], folds, m, blocks_act)
    else:
        cons = _consistency_provisional(m06a)

    # --- eligibility (M5 pass as-of k) ---
    elig = m05[["entity_id", "fold_id", "eligible"]].rename(columns={"eligible": "m5_eligible"})

    # --- M6a provenance/copyability + n_journeys + tier + alloc + G5 fields.
    #     active_pretest_folds = # active 14d pretest sub-splits (m06a active_blocks), NOT
    #     active_test_folds (post-test_start info -> look-ahead). G5 uses active_pretest_folds. ---
    m6a_cols = m06a[["entity_id", "fold_id", "copyable", "rankable", "n_journeys_pretest",
                     "m4_tier", "entity_alloc_weight", "active_blocks",
                     "g5_pool_candidate_pass"]].copy()
    m6a_cols = m6a_cols.rename(columns={"active_blocks": "active_pretest_folds"})

    # --- assemble: UNIVERSE = M6a (every shortlist row), LEFT-join M7 engine results. A M6a row with
    #     no M7 row stays in the output with roe_adj NaN -> excluded (missing_m07_row), never dropped. ---
    df = m6a_cols.merge(summ, on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
    df = df.merge(elig, on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
    df = df.merge(cons, on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
    df = df.merge(exposure, on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
    df["exposure_days"] = df["exposure_days"].fillna(0.0)
    df["consistency"] = df["consistency"].fillna(0.0)
    df["n_active_subsplits"] = df["n_active_subsplits"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    df["consistency_source"] = df["consistency_source"].fillna(
        "unavailable" if inputs["m07_equity"] is None else "m07_equity_block_roe")

    # --- M4 copyable from entities (KILL/no-primary -> not copyable). m6a copyable already encodes it
    #     but cross-check against m04_entities as the authority. ---
    if inputs.get("m04_fold_pure") and "fold_id" in m04e.columns:
        ent_copy = m04e[["entity_id", "fold_id", "copyable"]].rename(columns={"copyable": "entity_copyable"})
        df = df.merge(ent_copy, on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
    else:
        ent_copy = m04e[["entity_id", "copyable"]].rename(columns={"copyable": "entity_copyable"})
        df = df.merge(ent_copy, on="entity_id", how="left")
    df["entity_copyable"] = df["entity_copyable"].fillna(False)

    # --- fidelity: needs tracking_error from M7 summary. Absent column OR NaN/inf value -> UNKNOWN.
    #     Real fidelity only where tracking_error is finite (codex r2 #B: a present-but-NaN column
    #     must not pass as real; flagged PER ROW). FIX (v2): an unknown-fidelity row is filled with the
    #     FOLD MEAN of the known fidelities -- NOT the max (1.0). NaN->1.0 advantaged unknown rows
    #     (best-possible fidelity, a free top-bucket boost). Fold-mean is neutral within the fold (no
    #     advantage, no penalty). When a fold has NO known fidelities the term contributes 0 there.
    #     Provisional rows still block investability via fidelity_source regardless of the fill value. ---
    if "tracking_error" in df.columns:
        te = pd.to_numeric(df["tracking_error"], errors="coerce")
        te_finite = np.isfinite(te.to_numpy(dtype="float64"))  # rejects NaN AND +/-inf
        real_fid = (1.0 - (te / m.fidelity_B).clip(0, 1)).where(te_finite)
        df["fidelity"] = real_fid
        df["fidelity_source"] = np.where(te_finite, "m07_tracking_error", "unavailable_provisional")
        # fill unknown rows with the per-fold mean of KNOWN fidelities (neutral within fold)
        fold_mean = df.groupby("fold_id")["fidelity"].transform("mean")
        df["fidelity"] = df["fidelity"].fillna(fold_mean).fillna(0.0)
    else:
        # no tracking_error column at all -> no known fidelity anywhere -> term contributes 0.
        df["fidelity"] = 0.0
        df["fidelity_source"] = "unavailable_provisional"

    df["capacity_health"] = 1.0 - df["capacity_capped_frac"].clip(0, 1)
    nj = df["n_journeys_pretest"].clip(lower=1)
    df["survivability_penalty"] = ((df["n_backstop_transfer"] + 2 * df["account_ruin"]) / nj).clip(0, 1)
    df["calmar"] = df["roe_adj"] / df["max_dd_pretest"].clip(lower=m.dd_floor)

    # --- RANKABLE eligibility (design §3): M5-pass + M4-copyable + provenance OK + MIN-SUPPORT. ---
    # n_round_trips gate (codex finding): an entity with < MIN_ROUND_TRIPS CLOSED round-trips has
    # no meaningful realized after-cost return basis (M7 emits 0.0 realized metrics when
    # n_round_trips==0), so it must NOT be rankable / enter the pool. Applies whenever the engine
    # reported round-trip accounting (column present in the M7 summary); for entities present in M7
    # the NaN-or-below case fails. When the column is entirely absent (legacy/provisional input with
    # no round-trip accounting) the gate is a no-op (all True) so dry-runs still rank.
    n_round_trips = pd.to_numeric(df["n_round_trips"], errors="coerce")
    round_trip_gate = (n_round_trips >= MIN_ROUND_TRIPS) if has_n_round_trips \
        else pd.Series(True, index=df.index)
    rankable = (
        df["m5_eligible"].fillna(False)
        & df["entity_copyable"]
        & (df["n_fills"] >= m.min_fills_pretest)
        & (df["exposure_days"] >= m.min_exposure_days)
        & (df["n_active_subsplits"] >= m.min_active_subsplits_support)
        & round_trip_gate
        & df["roe_adj"].notna() & df["max_dd_pretest"].notna()
    )
    df["m6b_rankable"] = rankable
    excl = []
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        if r["m6b_rankable"]:
            excl.append("")
            continue
        reasons = []
        if not bool(r["m5_eligible"]):
            reasons.append("m5_ineligible")
        if not bool(r["entity_copyable"]):
            reasons.append("not_copyable")
        if r["n_fills"] < m.min_fills_pretest:
            reasons.append(f"n_fills<{m.min_fills_pretest}")
        if r["exposure_days"] < m.min_exposure_days:
            reasons.append(f"exposure<{m.min_exposure_days}d")
        if r["n_active_subsplits"] < m.min_active_subsplits_support:
            reasons.append("active_subsplits<2")
        if has_n_round_trips:
            _nrt = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r["n_round_trips"]]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            if pd.isna(_nrt) or _nrt < MIN_ROUND_TRIPS:
                reasons.append(f"n_round_trips<{MIN_ROUND_TRIPS}")
        if pd.isna(r["roe_adj"]) or pd.isna(r["max_dd_pretest"]):
            reasons.append("missing_m07_row")
        excl.append("|".join(reasons) or "excluded")
    df["excluded_reason"] = excl

    # --- per-fold winsorize + z-score over RANKABLE entities, then frozen score ---
    out_parts = []
    for fid, g in df.groupby("fold_id"):
        g = g.copy()
        rk = g[g["m6b_rankable"]].copy()
        if rk.empty:
            g["m6b_score"] = np.nan
            out_parts.append(g)
            continue
        rk["roe_adj_w"] = _winsorize(rk["roe_adj"], m.winsor_lo_pct, m.winsor_hi_pct)
        rk["calmar_w"] = _winsorize(rk["calmar"], m.winsor_lo_pct, m.winsor_hi_pct)
        rk["z_realized_roe"] = _zscore(rk["roe_adj_w"])
        rk["z_calmar"] = _zscore(rk["calmar_w"])
        # win-rate term (v2): z-scored per fold like the others. Unknown win-rate -> fold mean
        # (z=0, neutral) so a missing-realized row neither gains nor loses on this term.
        wr = pd.to_numeric(rk["round_trip_win_rate"], errors="coerce")
        wr = wr.fillna(wr.mean())
        rk["z_win_rate"] = _zscore(wr) if wr.notna().any() else 0.0
        rk["m6b_score"] = (
            m.w_realized_roe * rk["z_realized_roe"]
            + m.w_calmar * rk["z_calmar"]
            + m.w_win_rate * rk["z_win_rate"]
            + m.w_consistency * rk["consistency"]
            + m.w_capacity_health * rk["capacity_health"]
            + m.w_fidelity * rk["fidelity"]
            - m.w_survivability_penalty * rk["survivability_penalty"]
        )
        g = g.merge(rk[["entity_id", "fold_id", "z_realized_roe", "z_calmar", "z_win_rate",
                        "roe_adj_w", "calmar_w", "m6b_score"]],
                    on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
        out_parts.append(g)

   274	def _consistency_from_equity(equity: pd.DataFrame, folds: pd.DataFrame,
   275	                             m: M6bManifest, blocks_activity: Optional[pd.DataFrame]) -> pd.DataFrame:
   276	    """consistency = (# active sub-splits with block roe_adj>0) / (# active sub-splits).
   277	    A sub-split is ACTIVE iff >= min_journeys opened AND >= min_fills in it. Block roe_adj = engine
   278	    after-cost ROE on that block = eq[block_end]/eq[block_start] - 1.
   279	
   280	    NOTE: activeness needs per-block journey + fill counts. fills_blocks (per entity,fold,block ->
   281	    n_fills, n_journeys) supplies them; when unavailable we fall back to fill-count-only activeness
   282	    from the equity sample density (conservative: still requires the block to carry >=min_fills
   283	    equity-action samples).
   284	    """
   285	    rows = []
   286	    fold_win = {int(r.fold_id): (pd.Timestamp(r.train_start).value // 1_000_000,
   287	                                 pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000)
   288	                for r in folds.itertuples()}
   289	    block_ms = m.block_days * MS_PER_DAY
   290	    # (entity, fold, block) -> (n_fills_block, n_journeys_block) for the activeness test.
   291	    ba = {}
   292	    keys_act = set()
   293	    if blocks_activity is not None and not blocks_activity.empty:
   294	        for r in blocks_activity.itertuples():
   295	            ba[(int(r.entity_id), int(r.fold_id), int(r.block_idx))] = (
   296	                int(r.n_fills_block), int(r.n_journeys_block))
   297	            keys_act.add((int(r.entity_id), int(r.fold_id)))
   298	    # equity grouped for O(1) lookup; iterate the UNION of (entity,fold) from blocks_activity AND
   299	    # equity (codex r3 #1: an entity active by fills+journeys but with NO equity rows must still get
   300	    # active=N, positive=0, consistency=0 -- fail-closed, never silently absent).
   301	    eq_groups = {(int(k[0]), int(k[1])): g for k, g in equity.groupby(["entity_id", "fold_id"])} \
   302	        if not equity.empty else {}
   303	    all_keys = sorted(keys_act | set(eq_groups.keys()))  # sorted -> deterministic row order
   304	    for (eid, fid) in all_keys:
   305	        if int(fid) not in fold_win:
   306	            continue
   307	        t0, t1 = fold_win[int(fid)]
   308	        grp = eq_groups.get((int(eid), int(fid)))
   309	        anchor_ts = anchor_eq = None
   310	        if grp is not None and not grp.empty:
   311	            grp = grp.sort_values("ts")
   312	            ts = grp["ts"].to_numpy()
   313	            eq = grp["subaccount_equity"].to_numpy()
   314	            # D4: per-block ROE uses the EXACT boundary anchors (fold_start/block_boundary/fold_end),
   315	            # via nearest-at-or-before lookup -- NOT within-block first/last (which excludes the exact
   316	            # block-end sample). Fall back to within-block only when no anchors exist (synthetic equity).
   317	            if "event_flag" in grp.columns:
   318	                am = grp["event_flag"].isin(["fold_start", "block_boundary", "fold_end"]).to_numpy()
   319	                if am.any():
   320	                    anchor_ts = ts[am]
   321	                    anchor_eq = eq[am]
   322	        else:
   323	            ts = np.empty(0, dtype="int64")
   324	            eq = np.empty(0, dtype="float64")
   325	        # consecutive 14d blocks FROM train_start; drop trailing partial (<14d). The ACTIVE
   326	        # denominator is driven by _blocks_activity (fills+journeys), NOT by equity-sample presence
   327	        # (codex r2 #A: skipping equity-empty blocks biased consistency upward). A block that is
   328	        # active by nf/nj counts in the denominator; it is POSITIVE only if equity actually covers
   329	        # it AND its after-cost block ROE > 0 (an active block with no equity coverage = no
   330	        # measurable positive ROE = NOT positive; conservative, no leak).
   331	        n_full = int((t1 - t0) // block_ms)
   332	        active = 0
   333	        positive = 0
   334	        for b in range(n_full):
   335	            nf, nj = ba.get((int(eid), int(fid), b), (0, 0))
   336	            if nf < m.consistency_active_min_fills or nj < m.consistency_active_min_journeys:
   337	                continue  # block not active -> excluded from BOTH numerator and denominator
   338	            active += 1
   339	            b0 = t0 + b * block_ms
   340	            b1 = t0 + (b + 1) * block_ms
   341	            if anchor_ts is not None and anchor_ts.size:
   342	                # D4: anchor-to-anchor block ROE = eq_anchor(b1)/eq_anchor(b0) - 1.
   343	                i0 = int(np.searchsorted(anchor_ts, b0, side="right")) - 1
   344	                i1 = int(np.searchsorted(anchor_ts, b1, side="right")) - 1
   345	                if i0 >= 0 and i1 >= 0:
   346	                    e0, e1 = float(anchor_eq[i0]), float(anchor_eq[i1])
   347	                    if e0 > 0 and (e1 / e0 - 1.0) > 0:
   348	                        positive += 1
   349	            else:
   350	                mask = (ts >= b0) & (ts < b0 + block_ms)
   351	                if mask.any():
   352	                    eq_in = eq[mask]
   353	                    if eq_in[0] > 0 and (eq_in[-1] / eq_in[0] - 1.0) > 0:
   354	                        positive += 1
   355	        cons = 0.0 if active < m.consistency_min_active_subsplits else positive / active
   356	        rows.append({"entity_id": int(eid), "fold_id": int(fid),
   357	                     "consistency": cons, "n_active_subsplits": active,
   358	                     "consistency_source": "m07_equity_block_roe"})
   359	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
   360	
   361	
   362	def _consistency_provisional(m06a: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
   363	    """PROVISIONAL fallback when m07_equity is absent. Uses M6a source persistence
   364	    (active_blocks/possible_blocks) as a SOURCE proxy for cross-sub-split repeatability. Flagged
   365	    non-final: provisional M6b is investable=False, so this never reaches LP/live evidence."""
   366	    df = m06a[["entity_id", "fold_id", "active_blocks", "possible_blocks"]].copy()
   367	    poss = df["possible_blocks"].clip(lower=1)
   368	    df["consistency"] = (df["active_blocks"] / poss).clip(0, 1).fillna(0.0)
   369	    df["n_active_subsplits"] = df["active_blocks"].fillna(0).astype(int)
   370	    df["consistency_source"] = "m6a_source_persistence_PROVISIONAL"
   371	    return df[["entity_id", "fold_id", "consistency", "n_active_subsplits", "consistency_source"]]

   374	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   375	# SCORING
   376	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   377	def _winsorize(s: pd.Series, lo_pct: float, hi_pct: float) -> pd.Series:
   378	    if s.notna().sum() == 0:
   379	        return s
   380	    lo = np.nanpercentile(s, lo_pct)
   381	    hi = np.nanpercentile(s, hi_pct)
   382	    return s.clip(lower=lo, upper=hi)
   383	
   384	
   385	def _zscore(s: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
   386	    mu = s.mean()
   387	    sd = s.std(ddof=0)
   388	    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd == 0:
   389	        return pd.Series(np.zeros(len(s)), index=s.index)
   390	    return (s - mu) / sd

   393	def build_ranking(inputs: dict, m: M6bManifest) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
   394	    """Core M6b pipeline. Returns (pool_df, manifest_dict)."""
   395	    folds = inputs["folds"]
   396	    summ = inputs["m07_summary"].copy()
   397	    m06a = inputs["m06a"].copy()
   398	    m05 = inputs["m05"].copy()
   399	    m04e = inputs["m04_entities"].copy()
   400	
   401	    # --- provenance: per-fold pretest-pure. M6a/M5 carry as_of_ms; refuse any input stamped AFTER
   402	    #     test_start[k] (fixes r1#9 -- no inherited survivor bias / no leak). ---
   403	    fold_test_start_ms = {int(r.fold_id): pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000
   404	                          for r in folds.itertuples()}
   405	    provenance_fail = []
   406	    prov_inputs = [("m06a", m06a), ("m05", m05)]
   407	    if inputs.get("m04_fold_pure"):
   408	        prov_inputs.extend([("m04_entities", m04e), ("m04_auth", inputs["m04_auth"])])
   409	    for nm, df in prov_inputs:
   410	        if "as_of_ms" not in df.columns:
   411	            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:no_as_of_ms_column")
   412	            continue
   413	        if nm.startswith("m04") and "fold_id" not in df.columns:
   414	            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:no_fold_id_column")
   415	            continue
   416	        # require PRESENT + non-null as_of_ms for every row (missing provenance is not provenance-OK)
   417	        n_null = int(df["as_of_ms"].isna().sum())
   418	        if n_null:
   419	            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{n_null}_rows_null_as_of_ms")
   420	        ts_start = df["fold_id"].map(fold_test_start_ms)
   421	        if ts_start.isna().any():
   422	            provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(ts_start.isna().sum())}_rows_unknown_fold")
   423	        if nm.startswith("m04"):
   424	            bad = (df["as_of_ms"].astype("float64") != ts_start.astype("float64")).fillna(False)
   425	            if bad.any():
   426	                provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(bad.sum())}_rows_as_of_not_equal_test_start")
   427	        else:
   428	            bad = (df["as_of_ms"].astype("float64") > ts_start.astype("float64")).fillna(False)
   429	            if bad.any():
   430	                provenance_fail.append(f"{nm}:{int(bad.sum())}_rows_post_test_start")
   431	    if provenance_fail:
   432	        raise ValueError(f"M6b PROVENANCE FAIL-CLOSED: {provenance_fail}")
   433	
   434	    # --- engine after-cost results per (entity,fold) ---
   435	    summ = summ.rename(columns={"max_dd": "max_dd_pretest"})
   436	    summ["capacity_capped_frac"] = summ["n_capacity_capped"] / summ["n_actions"].clip(lower=1)
   437	    summ["account_ruin"] = summ["ruin"].astype(int)
   438	    # uncalibrated-cost flags -> drives provisional/FINAL
   439	    uncalibrated = bool(summ["slippage_uncalibrated"].any() or summ["fee_unversioned"].any())
   440	
   441	    # --- RETURN BASIS (v2): rank on M7 REALIZED round-trip ROE, NOT full-window MTM roe_engine.
   442	    #     The M7 engine emits realized_roe over CLEAN round-trips (realized_pnl_total / start_equity).
   443	    #     Fall back to roe_engine ONLY when realized_roe is absent, flagged per-run + per-row so a
   444	    #     fallback never silently masquerades as the realized basis. ---
   445	    has_realized = "realized_roe" in summ.columns
   446	    if has_realized:
   447	        realized = pd.to_numeric(summ["realized_roe"], errors="coerce")
   448	        summ["roe_adj"] = realized
   449	        # rows where realized_roe is NaN but roe_engine exists fall back per-row (flagged)
   450	        if "roe_engine" in summ.columns:
   451	            fb_mask = summ["roe_adj"].isna() & summ["roe_engine"].notna()
   452	            summ.loc[fb_mask, "roe_adj"] = pd.to_numeric(
   453	                summ.loc[fb_mask, "roe_engine"], errors="coerce")
   454	        summ["return_basis"] = np.where(realized.notna(), "realized_roe", "roe_engine_fallback")
   455	        return_basis_fallback = bool((summ["return_basis"] == "roe_engine_fallback").any())
   456	    else:
   457	        summ["roe_adj"] = pd.to_numeric(summ.get("roe_engine"), errors="coerce")
   458	        summ["return_basis"] = "roe_engine_fallback"
   459	        return_basis_fallback = True
   460	    # round-trip win-rate term (v2). Absent -> NaN (treated as missing realized metrics downstream).

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""V15 M6b -- Copyability-Adjusted FINAL Ranking (post-engine).
     2	
     3	Design (FROZEN): brain projects/quant/v15/modules/m06b (codex DESIGN-SHIP r3, Alberto greenlit
     4	2026-06-01). This module is the ONLY ranking that locks the V15 investable pool. It re-ranks the
     5	M6a high-recall shortlist on M7 AFTER-OUR-COST engine results computed FOLD-PRETEST-PURE on
     6	[train_start[k], test_start[k]) -- no OOS leak -- via a fully pre-registered spec, and emits the
     7	per-fold INVESTABLE POOL + a QUALITY-only weight. Raw source ROE never locks the pool (thesis 4b).
     8	
     9	SIZING CHAIN (one multiplier per module, applied in M9):
    10	  final_slice = quality_weight(M6b) x confidence(M4 alloc) x survival(M8 tier) x caps/anti-corr(M9)
    11	M6b owns ONLY quality_weight (does NOT multiply M4/M8 -- no double-count).
    12	
    13	PROVISIONAL vs FINAL (design §1): an M6b run on uncalibrated costs (slippage_uncalibrated or
    14	fee_unversioned True on its M7 input) is stamped investable=False (pipeline dry-run only). The FINAL
    15	investable pool reruns AFTER cost calibration, recording fee_schedule_version +
    16	slippage_calibration_version in the manifest.
    17	
    18	Run:
    19	  /Users/hermes/miniforge3/envs/quants-lab/bin/python research/v15/v15_m06b_ranking.py \
    20	      --m07-dir app/data/v15/m07_pretest --out app/data/v15
    21	"""
    22	from __future__ import annotations
    23	
    24	import argparse
    25	import json
    26	import logging
    27	from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
    28	from pathlib import Path
    29	from typing import Optional
    30	
    31	import numpy as np
    32	import pandas as pd
    33	
    34	logger = logging.getLogger("m06b")
    35	
    36	DATA_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15"
    37	MS_PER_DAY = 86_400_000
    38	
    39	# Pre-registered min CLOSED round-trips for an entity to be RANKABLE / pool-eligible (codex
    40	# finding): M7 emits realized_roe=0.0 / round_trip_win_rate=0.0 when n_round_trips==0, so an
    41	# open-only entity (zero closed round-trips) would otherwise look valid and rank/pool. The rank
    42	# basis is the after-cost REALIZED round-trip return; without enough closed round-trips that basis
    43	# is undefined/meaningless. Require >= this many closed round-trips to enter in_pool/quality_weight.
    44	MIN_ROUND_TRIPS = 5
    45	
    46	
    47	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    48	# FROZEN MANIFEST (design §3/§4/§5). Every constant here is pre-registered and
    49	# written verbatim into m06b_manifest.json BEFORE any OOS/M9/M10 run.
    50	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    51	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    52	class M6bManifest:
    53	    manifest_version: str = "m06b-v2"
    54	    # Score coefficients (relative importances; NOT required to sum to 1 -- mixed scales).
    55	    # v2: return basis switched to M7 REALIZED round-trip ROE (was full-window MTM roe_engine);
    56	    #     win-rate term ADDED. New positive-weight vector sums to 0.95 (1.00 - 0.15 - 0.10 net of
    57	    #     the subtracted survivability penalty kept at its prior magnitude):
    58	    #       0.25 z_realized_roe + 0.20 z_calmar + 0.15 z_win_rate + 0.15 consistency
    59	    #       + 0.10 capacity_health + 0.10 fidelity - 0.15 survivability_penalty
    60	    w_realized_roe: float = 0.25
    61	    w_calmar: float = 0.20
    62	    w_win_rate: float = 0.15
    63	    w_consistency: float = 0.15
    64	    w_capacity_health: float = 0.10
    65	    w_fidelity: float = 0.10
    66	    w_survivability_penalty: float = 0.15  # subtracted
    67	    # calmar / winsor / fidelity
    68	    dd_floor: float = 0.05            # calmar = realized_roe / max(max_dd, dd_floor)
    69	    winsor_lo_pct: float = 1.0        # winsorize calmar + realized_roe at fold [p1, p99]
    70	    winsor_hi_pct: float = 99.0
    71	    fidelity_B: float = 0.25          # fidelity = 1 - clamp(tracking_error / B, 0, 1)  (M7 D5)
    72	    # consistency sub-split geometry (design §3): consecutive 14d blocks FROM train_start; trailing
    73	    # partial (<14d) DROPPED; sub-split ACTIVE iff >=1 journey opened AND >=5 fills in it.
    74	    block_days: int = 14
    75	    consistency_active_min_journeys: int = 1
    76	    consistency_active_min_fills: int = 5
    77	    consistency_min_active_subsplits: int = 2  # < this -> consistency = 0, not top-bucket eligible
    78	    # min-support to be RANKABLE
    79	    min_fills_pretest: int = 30
    80	    min_exposure_days: float = 3.0
    81	    min_active_subsplits_support: int = 2
    82	    # pool selection + G5
    83	    n_pool: int = 100
    84	    g5_min_active_pretest_folds: int = 3
    85	    g5_min_journeys_pretest: int = 5
    86	    # allocation -- quality weight only
    87	    bucket_weights: tuple = (5, 4, 3, 2, 1)   # quintile buckets, normalized to sum 1
    88	    n_buckets: int = 5
    89	    top_bucket_consistency_gate: float = 0.5  # bucket-1 entity with consistency < this -> demote 1
    90	    per_entity_quality_ceiling: float = 0.10  # pre-M9 ceiling = 10% of pool total
    91	    # provenance / provisional
    92	    informed_by: str = "pretest-distribution-only"
    93	    fee_schedule_version: Optional[str] = None
    94	    slippage_calibration_version: Optional[str] = None
    95	
    96	
    97	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    98	# INPUT LOADING (design §2: fold-pretest-pure; provenance fail-closed)
    99	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   100	def _read_parquet_maybe_parts(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
   101	    """Read a parquet that may be a single file OR a `<name>.parts/` dir of shards (streaming I/O)."""
   102	    parts = path.parent / (path.name + ".parts")
   103	    if path.exists() and path.is_file():
   104	        return pd.read_parquet(path)
   105	    if parts.exists() and parts.is_dir():
   106	        shards = sorted(parts.glob("*.parquet"))
   107	        if not shards:
   108	            return pd.DataFrame()
   109	        return pd.concat((pd.read_parquet(s) for s in shards), ignore_index=True)
   110	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"neither {path} nor {parts} exists")
   111	
   112	
   113	def _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir: Path, folds: pd.DataFrame, stem: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
   114	    parts = []
   115	    for fid in sorted(folds["fold_id"].astype(int).unique()):
   116	        p = m04_dir / f"{stem}_f{fid}.parquet"
   117	        if not p.exists():
   118	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"missing fold-pure M4 file for fold {fid}: {p}")
   119	        df = pd.read_parquet(p).copy()
   120	        df["fold_id"] = int(fid)
   121	        parts.append(df)
   122	    return pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True) if parts else pd.DataFrame()
   123	
   124	
   125	def load_inputs(m07_dir: Path, data_dir: Path = DATA_DIR, m04_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict:
   126	    """Load the M7 pretest-window engine results + upstream M6a/M5/M4/M3 inputs.
   127	
   128	    Returns a dict of DataFrames. Provenance fail-closed checks happen in build_ranking once we
   129	    know each fold's test_start.
   130	    """
   131	    folds = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m03_folds.parquet")
   132	
   133	    m07_summary = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(m07_dir / "m07_summary.parquet")
   134	    # m07_fills only needed for exposure_time (per (entity,fold) our_ts span). Read lazily/aggregated.
   135	    fills_path = m07_dir / "m07_fills.parquet"
   136	
   137	    m06a = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m06a_shortlist.parquet")
   138	    m05 = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m05_eligibility.parquet")
   139	    if m04_dir is None:
   140	        logger.warning("LOUD WARNING: using single M4 authenticity/entities files for all folds; "
   141	                       "M6b M4 consumption is not fold-pure. Pass --m04-dir.")
   142	        m04_entities = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m04_entities.parquet")
   143	        # wallet -> entity_id map (for per-block journey-open counts from m02 when equity is present)
   144	        m04_auth = pd.read_parquet(data_dir / "m04_authenticity.parquet")
   145	        m04_fold_pure = False
   146	    else:
   147	        m04_entities = _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir, folds, "m04_entities")
   148	        m04_auth = _load_m04_by_fold(m04_dir, folds, "m04_authenticity")
   149	        m04_fold_pure = True
   150	    m02_journeys_path = data_dir / "m02_journeys.parquet"
   151	
   152	    # optional: per-action equity path (FINAL consistency source). Absent in current shipped runner.
   153	    eq_path = m07_dir / "m07_equity.parquet"
   154	    m07_equity = None
   155	    try:
   156	        m07_equity = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(eq_path)
   157	        if m07_equity.empty:
   158	            m07_equity = None
   159	    except FileNotFoundError:
   160	        m07_equity = None
   161	
   162	    return {
   163	        "folds": folds,
   164	        "m07_summary": m07_summary,
   165	        "m07_fills_path": fills_path,
   166	        "m06a": m06a,
   167	        "m05": m05,
   168	        "m04_entities": m04_entities,
   169	        "m04_auth": m04_auth,
   170	        "m04_fold_pure": m04_fold_pure,
   171	        "m02_journeys_path": m02_journeys_path,
   172	        "m07_equity": m07_equity,
   173	    }
   174	
   175	
   176	def _exposure_days_from_fills(fills_path: Path, m07_dir: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
   177	    """Per (entity_id, fold_id) exposure_time in days = (max our_ts - min our_ts)/day, from the M7
   178	    fills stream. Single streamed groupby; reads only the needed columns."""
   179	    df = _read_parquet_maybe_parts(fills_path)
   180	    if df.empty:
   181	        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["entity_id", "fold_id", "exposure_days"])
   182	    g = df.groupby(["entity_id", "fold_id"])["our_ts"].agg(["min", "max"]).reset_index()
   183	    g["exposure_days"] = (g["max"] - g["min"]) / MS_PER_DAY
   184	    return g[["entity_id", "fold_id", "exposure_days"]]
   185	
   186	
   187	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   188	# CONSISTENCY (design §3): per-sub-split (14d block from train_start) ENGINE after-cost ROE.
   189	# FINAL source = m07_equity per-block ROE. PROVISIONAL fallback = M6a source persistence (clearly
   190	# flagged non-final; provisional outputs are investable=False anyway).
   191	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   192	def _blocks_activity(fills_path, m02_journeys_path: Path, m04_auth: pd.DataFrame,
   193	                     folds: pd.DataFrame, m: M6bManifest) -> pd.DataFrame:
   194	    """Per (entity_id, fold_id, block_idx) -> n_fills + n_journeys, for the consistency activeness
   195	    test (active iff >= min_journeys opened AND >= min_fills in the block). Blocks are consecutive
   196	    14d windows FROM train_start within the pretest [train_start, test_start); trailing partial
   197	    (block_idx >= n_full) is excluded by the consumer. Only computed when m07_equity is present
   198	    (FINAL path); the provisional path uses M6a source persistence and does not call this.
   199	
   200	    fills_path: m07 fills (entity_id, fold_id, our_ts) -> per-block n_fills (engine fills).
   201	    m02 journeys (wallet, entry_ts) mapped to entity via m04_auth (wallet->entity_id) -> per-block
   202	    journey OPENS (entry_ts in block), assigned to every fold whose pretest window contains entry_ts.
   203	    """
   204	    block_ms = m.block_days * MS_PER_DAY
   205	    fold_win = [(int(r.fold_id), pd.Timestamp(r.train_start).value // 1_000_000,
   206	                 pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).value // 1_000_000) for r in folds.itertuples()]
   207	
   208	    # --- fills per (entity, fold, block) ---
   209	    fdf = fills_path if isinstance(fills_path, pd.DataFrame) else _read_parquet_maybe_parts(fills_path)
   210	    f_rows = []
   211	    if not fdf.empty:
   212	        fdf = fdf[["entity_id", "fold_id", "our_ts"]].copy()
   213	        for fid, t0, t1 in fold_win:
   214	            sub = fdf[fdf.fold_id == fid]
   215	            if sub.empty:
   216	                continue
   217	            blk = ((sub["our_ts"] - t0) // block_ms).astype(int)
   218	            sub = sub.assign(block_idx=blk)
   219	            sub = sub[(sub["our_ts"] >= t0) & (sub["our_ts"] < t1)]
   220	            g = sub.groupby(["entity_id", "block_idx"]).size().reset_index(name="n_fills_block")

   521	    #     must not pass as real; flagged PER ROW). FIX (v2): an unknown-fidelity row is filled with the
   522	    #     FOLD MEAN of the known fidelities -- NOT the max (1.0). NaN->1.0 advantaged unknown rows
   523	    #     (best-possible fidelity, a free top-bucket boost). Fold-mean is neutral within the fold (no
   524	    #     advantage, no penalty). When a fold has NO known fidelities the term contributes 0 there.
   525	    #     Provisional rows still block investability via fidelity_source regardless of the fill value. ---
   526	    if "tracking_error" in df.columns:
   527	        te = pd.to_numeric(df["tracking_error"], errors="coerce")
   528	        te_finite = np.isfinite(te.to_numpy(dtype="float64"))  # rejects NaN AND +/-inf
   529	        real_fid = (1.0 - (te / m.fidelity_B).clip(0, 1)).where(te_finite)
   530	        df["fidelity"] = real_fid
   531	        df["fidelity_source"] = np.where(te_finite, "m07_tracking_error", "unavailable_provisional")
   532	        # fill unknown rows with the per-fold mean of KNOWN fidelities (neutral within fold)
   533	        fold_mean = df.groupby("fold_id")["fidelity"].transform("mean")
   534	        df["fidelity"] = df["fidelity"].fillna(fold_mean).fillna(0.0)
   535	    else:
   536	        # no tracking_error column at all -> no known fidelity anywhere -> term contributes 0.
   537	        df["fidelity"] = 0.0
   538	        df["fidelity_source"] = "unavailable_provisional"
   539	
   540	    df["capacity_health"] = 1.0 - df["capacity_capped_frac"].clip(0, 1)
   541	    nj = df["n_journeys_pretest"].clip(lower=1)
   542	    df["survivability_penalty"] = ((df["n_backstop_transfer"] + 2 * df["account_ruin"]) / nj).clip(0, 1)
   543	    df["calmar"] = df["roe_adj"] / df["max_dd_pretest"].clip(lower=m.dd_floor)
   544	
   545	    # --- RANKABLE eligibility (design §3): M5-pass + M4-copyable + provenance OK + MIN-SUPPORT. ---
   546	    # n_round_trips gate (codex finding): an entity with < MIN_ROUND_TRIPS CLOSED round-trips has

   571	        if not bool(r["m5_eligible"]):
   572	            reasons.append("m5_ineligible")
   573	        if not bool(r["entity_copyable"]):
   574	            reasons.append("not_copyable")
   575	        if r["n_fills"] < m.min_fills_pretest:
   576	            reasons.append(f"n_fills<{m.min_fills_pretest}")

   586	            reasons.append("missing_m07_row")

   591	    out_parts = []
   592	    for fid, g in df.groupby("fold_id"):
   593	        g = g.copy()
   594	        rk = g[g["m6b_rankable"]].copy()

   596	            g["m6b_score"] = np.nan
   597	            out_parts.append(g)
   598	            continue
   599	        rk["roe_adj_w"] = _winsorize(rk["roe_adj"], m.winsor_lo_pct, m.winsor_hi_pct)
   600	        rk["calmar_w"] = _winsorize(rk["calmar"], m.winsor_lo_pct, m.winsor_hi_pct)
   601	        rk["z_realized_roe"] = _zscore(rk["roe_adj_w"])
   602	        rk["z_calmar"] = _zscore(rk["calmar_w"])
   603	        # win-rate term (v2): z-scored per fold like the others. Unknown win-rate -> fold mean
   604	        # (z=0, neutral) so a missing-realized row neither gains nor loses on this term.
   605	        wr = pd.to_numeric(rk["round_trip_win_rate"], errors="coerce")
   606	        wr = wr.fillna(wr.mean())
   607	        rk["z_win_rate"] = _zscore(wr) if wr.notna().any() else 0.0
   608	        rk["m6b_score"] = (
   609	            m.w_realized_roe * rk["z_realized_roe"]
   610	            + m.w_calmar * rk["z_calmar"]
   611	            + m.w_win_rate * rk["z_win_rate"]
   612	            + m.w_consistency * rk["consistency"]
   613	            + m.w_capacity_health * rk["capacity_health"]
   614	            + m.w_fidelity * rk["fidelity"]
   615	            - m.w_survivability_penalty * rk["survivability_penalty"]
   616	        )
   617	        g = g.merge(rk[["entity_id", "fold_id", "z_realized_roe", "z_calmar", "z_win_rate",
   618	                        "roe_adj_w", "calmar_w", "m6b_score"]],
   619	                    on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
   620	        out_parts.append(g)
   621	    df = pd.concat(out_parts, ignore_index=True)
   622	
   623	    # --- POOL SELECTION + G5 (design §4) + allocation buckets (design §5) ---
   624	    pool_parts = []
   625	    for fid, g in df.groupby("fold_id"):
   626	        g = g.copy()
   627	        g["rank_in_fold"] = np.nan
   628	        g["in_pool"] = False
   629	        g["bucket"] = np.nan
   630	        g["quality_weight"] = 0.0
   631	        rk = g[g["m6b_rankable"] & g["m6b_score"].notna()].copy()
   632	        if rk.empty:
   633	            pool_parts.append(g)
   634	            continue
   635	        # deterministic ordering: score desc, then tie-break consistency desc, n_fills desc, entity asc
   636	        rk = rk.sort_values(
   637	            ["m6b_score", "consistency", "n_fills", "entity_id"],
   638	            ascending=[False, False, False, True]).reset_index(drop=True)
   639	        rk["rank_in_fold"] = np.arange(1, len(rk) + 1)
   640	        # G5 enforced HERE: active_pretest_folds>=3 AND n_journeys_pretest>=5 (pretest-only; no leak)
   641	        g5 = (rk["active_pretest_folds"].fillna(0) >= m.g5_min_active_pretest_folds) & \
   642	             (rk["n_journeys_pretest"].fillna(0) >= m.g5_min_journeys_pretest)
   643	        rk_g5 = rk[g5].copy()
   644	        pooled = rk_g5.head(m.n_pool).copy()
   645	        pooled["in_pool"] = True
   646	        # quintile buckets by score (equal-count). bucket 1 = top.
   647	        n = len(pooled)
   648	        if n > 0:
   649	            # qcut into n_buckets equal-count groups; labels 1..5 with 1 = highest score.
   650	            try:
   651	                q = pd.qcut(pooled["m6b_score"].rank(method="first"), m.n_buckets,
   652	                            labels=list(range(m.n_buckets, 0, -1)))
   653	                pooled["bucket"] = q.astype(int)
   654	            except ValueError:
   655	                # too few distinct -> linear split
   656	                pooled["bucket"] = (np.floor(
   657	                    (pooled["rank_in_fold"].rank(method="first") - 1) / max(n / m.n_buckets, 1)
   658	                ).astype(int) + 1).clip(1, m.n_buckets)
   659	            # TOP-BUCKET CONSISTENCY GATE: bucket-1 entity with consistency < gate -> demote 1 bucket
   660	            # (to 2). No backfill of bucket 1; no boundary recompute (deterministic, non-circular).
   661	            demote = (pooled["bucket"] == 1) & (pooled["consistency"] < m.top_bucket_consistency_gate)
   662	            pooled.loc[demote, "bucket"] = 2
   663	            # bucket weights [5,4,3,2,1] -> normalized to sum 1
   664	            bw = {i + 1: m.bucket_weights[i] for i in range(m.n_buckets)}
   665	            pooled["raw_w"] = pooled["bucket"].map(bw).astype(float)
   666	            tot = pooled["raw_w"].sum()
   667	            pooled["quality_weight"] = pooled["raw_w"] / tot if tot > 0 else 0.0
   668	            # per-entity ceiling = 10% of pool total; clip + renormalize remaining mass. For small
   669	            # pools where n * ceiling < 1 the nominal 10% is INFEASIBLE (weights could never sum to
   670	            # 1) -> use an effective ceiling = max(ceiling, 1/n) so a normalized solution always
   671	            # exists. Flagged in the manifest when the ceiling was relaxed.
   672	            ceil = max(m.per_entity_quality_ceiling, 1.0 / n)
   673	            ceiling_relaxed = ceil > m.per_entity_quality_ceiling + 1e-12
   674	            qw = np.array(pooled["quality_weight"].to_numpy(dtype=float), copy=True)
   675	            for _ in range(200):
   676	                over = qw > ceil + 1e-12
   677	                if not over.any():
   678	                    break
   679	                excess = (qw[over] - ceil).sum()
   680	                qw[over] = ceil
   681	                under = ~over
   682	                if qw[under].sum() <= 0:
   683	                    break
   684	                qw[under] += excess * qw[under] / qw[under].sum()
   685	            pooled["quality_weight"] = qw
   686	            # invariants: normalized to 1 and no weight exceeds the effective ceiling.
   687	            assert abs(qw.sum() - 1.0) < 1e-6, f"fold {fid}: quality_weight sum {qw.sum()} != 1"
   688	            assert qw.max() <= ceil + 1e-9, f"fold {fid}: weight {qw.max()} > ceiling {ceil}"
   689	            pooled["ceiling_relaxed"] = bool(ceiling_relaxed)
   690	        # merge pooled fields back
   691	        keep = ["entity_id", "fold_id", "rank_in_fold", "in_pool", "bucket", "quality_weight"]
   692	        g = g.drop(columns=["rank_in_fold", "in_pool", "bucket", "quality_weight"]).merge(
   693	            pooled[keep], on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
   694	        g["in_pool"] = g["in_pool"].fillna(False)
   695	        g["quality_weight"] = g["quality_weight"].fillna(0.0)
   696	        # rank_in_fold for non-pooled rankable: keep from rk
   697	        g = g.merge(rk[["entity_id", "fold_id", "rank_in_fold"]].rename(
   698	            columns={"rank_in_fold": "_rk2"}), on=["entity_id", "fold_id"], how="left")
   699	        g["rank_in_fold"] = g["rank_in_fold"].fillna(g["_rk2"])
   700	        g = g.drop(columns=["_rk2"])
   701	        pool_parts.append(g)
   702	    out = pd.concat(pool_parts, ignore_index=True)
   703	
   704	    # --- investable flag (provisional vs FINAL). FINAL (investable=True) requires ALL of:
   705	    #     calibrated costs (no uncalibrated flags) + fee/slippage calibration versions set +
   706	    #     REAL fidelity from M7 tracking_error (no neutral default) + REAL consistency from M7
   707	    #     after-cost equity block ROE (no M6a source-persistence proxy). Any provisional input ->
   708	    #     non-investable (the proxies are gameable / not after-our-cost). ---
   709	    # source flags gate FINAL on a PER-ROW basis over the RANKABLE rows (mode is fragile: a few
   710	    # provisional rows must still block). FINAL requires EVERY rankable row to carry real M7 sources.
   711	    rk_mask = out["m6b_rankable"].fillna(False)
   712	    # report the source over RANKABLE rows (the rows that gate FINAL); the global mode is dominated by
   713	    # non-rankable rows (no M7 row -> provisional default) and is misleading.
   714	    _src_rows = out.loc[rk_mask] if rk_mask.any() else out
   715	    consistency_source = _src_rows["consistency_source"].mode().iat[0] if len(_src_rows) else "unknown"
   716	    fidelity_source = _src_rows["fidelity_source"].mode().iat[0] if len(_src_rows) else "unknown"
   717	    fidelity_final = bool(rk_mask.any() and out.loc[rk_mask, "fidelity_source"].eq("m07_tracking_error").all())
   718	    consistency_final = bool(rk_mask.any() and out.loc[rk_mask, "consistency_source"].eq("m07_equity_block_roe").all())
   719	    # v2: FINAL additionally requires the REALIZED round-trip metrics to be present on every rankable
   720	    #     row -- a realized_roe (no roe_engine fallback) AND a finite round_trip_win_rate AND a
   721	    #     non-null n_round_trips. The rank now depends on realized round-trips; a fallback/missing
   722	    #     basis means the pool was not locked on the after-cost realized return.
   723	    if rk_mask.any():
   724	        realized_final = bool(
   725	            out.loc[rk_mask, "return_basis"].eq("realized_roe").all()
   726	            and pd.to_numeric(out.loc[rk_mask, "round_trip_win_rate"], errors="coerce").notna().all()
   727	            and pd.to_numeric(out.loc[rk_mask, "n_round_trips"], errors="coerce").notna().all()
   728	        )
   729	    else:
   730	        realized_final = False
   731	    investable = (
   732	        (not uncalibrated)
   733	        and (m.slippage_calibration_version is not None)
   734	        and (m.fee_schedule_version is not None)
   735	        and fidelity_final
   736	        and consistency_final
   737	        and realized_final
   738	    )
   739	    out["investable"] = bool(investable)
   740	    out["slippage_uncalibrated"] = summ["slippage_uncalibrated"].any() if "slippage_uncalibrated" in summ else True
   741	
   742	    return_basis = _src_rows["return_basis"].mode().iat[0] if (
   743	        "return_basis" in _src_rows and len(_src_rows)) else "unknown"
   744	    manifest = asdict(m)
   745	    manifest["uncalibrated_input"] = uncalibrated
   746	    manifest["investable"] = bool(investable)
   747	    manifest["consistency_source"] = consistency_source
   748	    manifest["fidelity_source"] = fidelity_source
   749	    manifest["return_basis"] = return_basis
   750	    manifest["return_basis_fallback"] = bool(return_basis_fallback)
   751	    if not investable:
   752	        reasons = []
   753	        if uncalibrated:
   754	            reasons.append("uncalibrated_costs")
   755	        if m.slippage_calibration_version is None:
   756	            reasons.append("no_slippage_calibration_version")
   757	        if m.fee_schedule_version is None:
   758	            reasons.append("no_fee_schedule_version")
   759	        if not fidelity_final:
   760	            reasons.append(f"fidelity_provisional({fidelity_source})")

  1627	    # "pretest" = [train_start,test_start) (M6b fold-pure ranking input). Same shipped engine; only the
  1628	    # window differs. start_ts_ms anchors risk at the window start.
  1629	    if window == "pretest":
  1630	        fold_win = {int(r.fold_id): (int(pd.Timestamp(r.train_start).timestamp() * 1000),
  1631	                                     int(pd.Timestamp(r.test_start).timestamp() * 1000))

  1633	    else:

  1635	                                     int(pd.Timestamp(r.test_end_excl).timestamp() * 1000))
  1636	                    for r in folds.itertuples()}
  1637	
  1638	    sl = pd.read_parquet(shortlist_path, columns=["entity_id", "primary_wallet", "fold_id", "in_shortlist"])
  1639	    sl = sl[sl.in_shortlist].copy()

  1647	    logger.info("M7 runner: %d seats over %d wallets", len(sl), len(wallets))
  1648	
  1649	    # PRE-SHARD actions by wallet ONCE via pyarrow write_dataset (codex code-r2 #4): streaming,
  1650	    # memory-bounded (no per-wallet writer held open buffering rows), FRESH dir each run (no stale
  1651	    # shard contamination). Hive-partitioned by wallet.

  1653	    if shard_dir.exists():
  1654	        shutil.rmtree(shard_dir)
  1655	    shard_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1656	    dataset = ds.dataset(actions_path, format="parquet")

   792	                len(out), n_pooled, pool_path, man_path)
   793	    return {"pool_rows": len(out), "pooled": n_pooled, "investable": manifest["investable"]}

   788	def _new_summary(entity_id, fold_id, start_equity, n_actions, params, md):

   795	        "slippage_band": params.slippage_band, "start_policy": params.start_policy,
   796	        "sizing_mode": params.sizing_mode,
   797	        "fixed_target_exposure": (
   798	            float(params.fixed_target_exposure) if params.sizing_mode == "fixed_position" else None
   799	        ),
   800	        "slippage_uncalibrated": False, "metadata_uncertain": False, "mode_uncertain": False,
   801	        "adv_unavailable": False,
   802	        "fee_unversioned": (not md.fees.versioned), "ruin": False,
   803	        "slippage_calibration_version": None,
   804	        # CHANGE 2 tracking_error accumulators (finalized in _finalize): time-weighted L1 signed
   805	        # portfolio-vector error over ACTIVE intervals + the active-time denominator.
   806	        "_te_weighted_sum": 0.0, "tracking_error_active_ms": 0,
   807	        # CHANGE 1: final_equity/roe_engine come from the ORIGINAL sample sites (action + non-ruin
   808	        # fold_end) ONLY -> the new boundary/ruin-drain samples never change them (byte-identical).
   809	        "_core_final_eq": float(start_equity),
   810	        # CHANGE A (round-trip aggregates for M6b): realized PnL after OUR costs per CLOSED round-trip
   811	        # (a coin's position opening from flat and returning to ~0). `_rt` holds the per-coin open
   812	        # accumulator (realized + funding - fees) since the position last opened from flat; finalized
   813	        # into the n_round_trips/wins/realized_pnl_total totals when the position closes. Popped in
   814	        # _finalize. Latency cost is ALREADY priced into each fill_px (CHANGE B), so the accumulated
   815	        # realized PnL is genuinely after fees+funding+latency.
   816	        "_rt": {}, "n_round_trips": 0, "n_round_trip_wins": 0, "realized_pnl_total": 0.0,
   817	    }
   818	
   819	
   820	def _eq_sample(entity_id, fold_id, ts, eq, flag, st, dd):
   821	    return {"entity_id": entity_id, "fold_id": fold_id, "ts": ts, "subaccount_equity": eq,
   822	            "event_flag": flag, "n_open_positions": len(st.positions), "dd_from_peak": dd}
   823	
   824	
   825	def _bar_vol_proxy(md: MarketData, coin: str, ts_ms: int) -> float:
   826	    """CHANGE B helper (CAUSAL FIX, codex finding #3): an UNSIGNED causal volatility proxy = the
   827	    |return| of the PRIOR COMPLETED 1m bar (bar_open <= ts - 60s), i.e. the last bar that has closed
   828	    BEFORE our fill timestamp. Using the CONTAINING bar's (close-open) was non-causal (within-minute
   829	    future info / look-ahead). The prior bar is fully observable before our_ts, so it removes the
   830	    look-ahead while preserving the discriminating power: high prior-bar vol = scalper/UHFT territory
   831	    is still penalized. This is a conservative CAUSAL VOL-PROXY APPROXIMATION of the unknown realized
   832	    within-minute drift our late fill eats, pending real sub-minute (HL trades / L2) data. Always >= 0
   833	    (magnitude only); the adverse SIGN is applied by the caller via the order side. NaN if the prior
   834	    bar is uncovered/zero-priced (-> no haircut)."""
   835	    mins, o, h, low, c = md.ohlc(coin)

 succeeded in 0ms:
   930	    skip and is NOT downsized by the participation/capacity cap. Exit impact is charged on the FULL
   931	    order/ADV ratio; capping impact at the entry participation limit would make emergency exits
   932	    optimistically cheap exactly when capacity is breached."""
   933	    if abs(delta_szi) < 1e-15:
   934	        return
   935	    szdec = md.meta.szdec(coin)
   936	    delta_szi = round(delta_szi, szdec)
   937	    if abs(delta_szi) < 10 ** (-szdec):
   938	        return
   939	    delta_notional = abs(delta_szi) * mark
   940	    if delta_notional < MIN_ORDER_NOTIONAL and not force_close:
   941	        return
   942	
   943	    mode = default_margin_mode(coin)
   944	    liq = md.liquidity(coin, our_ts)
   945	    if liq.get("adv_unavailable") and not force_close:
   946	        summary["adv_unavailable"] = True
   947	        summary["n_rejected"] += 1
   948	        return
   949	    if liq.get("uncalibrated"):
   950	        summary["slippage_uncalibrated"] = True
   951	    elif getattr(md, "slip_calib_version", None) is not None:
   952	        summary["slippage_calibration_version"] = md.slip_calib_version
   953	    adv = liq.get("adv", 0.0)
   954	    capacity_capped = False
   955	    if adv > 0 and not force_close:
   956	        max_notional = CAPACITY_PARTICIPATION_CAP * adv
   957	        if delta_notional > max_notional:
   958	            delta_szi *= max_notional / delta_notional
   959	            delta_szi = round(delta_szi, szdec)
   960	            delta_notional = abs(delta_szi) * mark
   961	            capacity_capped = True
   962	            summary["n_capacity_capped"] += 1
   963	            if delta_notional < MIN_ORDER_NOTIONAL:
   964	                return
   965

    20	def _folds(n_folds=1):
    21	    rows = []
    22	    base = pd.Timestamp("2025-12-01")
    23	    for k in range(1, n_folds + 1):
    24	        train_start = base + pd.Timedelta(days=14 * (k - 1))
    25	        test_start = train_start + pd.Timedelta(days=56)   # 42 train + 14 val = 4 x 14d blocks
    26	        rows.append({
    27	            "fold_id": k, "train_start": train_start, "test_start": test_start,
    28	            "test_end_excl": test_start + pd.Timedelta(days=14),
    29	        })
    30	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)

    33	def _make_inputs(n_entities=120, fold_id=1, *, uncalibrated=True, equity=None,
    34	                 eligible_all=True, copyable_all=True, n_fills=100, exposure_days=10.0,
    35	                 n_journeys=10, active_pretest_folds=3, consistency_active=3,
    36	                 tracking_error=None, realized=False):
    37	    folds = _folds(1)
    38	    ts_asof = pd.Timestamp(folds.loc[0, "test_start"]).value // 1_000_000 - 1000
    39	    ids = list(range(n_entities))

    41	    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    42	    summ = pd.DataFrame({

    49	        "ruin": False,
    50	        "final_equity": 12000.0,
    51	        "roe_engine": rng.normal(0.2, 0.5, n_entities),
    52	        "slippage_uncalibrated": uncalibrated, "fee_unversioned": uncalibrated,
    53	        "metadata_uncertain": False, "mode_uncertain": False,
    54	    })
    55	    if tracking_error is not None:
    56	        summ["tracking_error"] = float(tracking_error)
    57	    if realized:
    58	        # M7 v2 realized round-trip keys. realized_roe is the rank basis; round_trip_win_rate is the
    59	        # new win-rate term. Make them deterministic + distinct from roe_engine so tests can assert
    60	        # the score consumes the realized basis.
    61	        summ["realized_roe"] = rng.normal(0.15, 0.4, n_entities)
    62	        summ["n_round_trips"] = rng.integers(5, 50, n_entities)

    64	        summ["round_trip_win_rate"] = summ["n_round_trip_wins"] / summ["n_round_trips"].clip(lower=1)
    65	        summ["realized_pnl_total"] = summ["realized_roe"] * summ["start_equity"]
    66	    m06a = pd.DataFrame({
    67	        "entity_id": ids, "fold_id": fold_id, "primary_wallet": wallets,
    68	        "copyable": copyable_all, "rankable": True,
